CONDABRA
A/C COMMANDANT
In this
--- seg-a0a630a2592128022c975f3a [needs-review] ---
On June 1, 1995, we initiated the writing of a letter (a copy of which is attached) regarding No. 95/113, but we did not receive a written response, which we would like this Command to provide.

On the other hand, we have initiated an excellent telephone relationship with Col. Coelho and Col. Ortiz, who are working on a project aimed at cataloging and exchanging information with international groups interested in the phenomenon of UFO?0 or OVNI.

As we agreed, we have sent 3 informative bulletins (SUPYSÁUA Nos. 37, 38, and 39). We hope you will appreciate the content of these bulletins. We note that the cases published in this journal have the final goal of informing the interested public, clearly, simply, and objectively. The bulletin is not too technical, but rather accessible to a broad audience, unlike our research, which is based on a methodology founded in the reality of Physics that surrounds us.

On April 5, we were in Varginha, MG, participating in...
--- seg-864026b6eeb8de18f221a09d [needs-review] ---
From an important meeting, which brought together several UFO groups with *
some military personnel. The main topic of the meeting revolved around the capture
of "strange creatures." Part of this episode was disclosed by the press and media at local,
national, and international levels. In this context, according to some witnesses, the Fire
Department, Civil/Military Police, and the Army were involved.

Shortly after my return from Varginha, I made contact with Col. Coelho, informing him
that our group had been tasked with drafting a letter to CONDABRA, requesting his
intervention regarding this case. Col. Coelho advised us to send the letter directly to
V.Sa., who would take the necessary actions to clarify the matter.

We would like to know if there is a policy of cover-up in this case or in facts related
to the UFO or OVNI phenomenon, which is cataloged by this Command.

We await your response; we communicate that we are open to dialogue and at your
disposal for any information regarding our studies.

Regards.

0 CASE OF
The extraordinary report
of an alien contact
mobilizes ufologists and

--- seg-535974b62856b361a65b1a13 [machine-unreviewed] ---
Luiza Villamea
--- seg-ae8ece266c6102eb103be149 [needs-review] ---
From Varginha

A recent economic filon from the city of Varginha, Minas Gerais, a known coffee exporter, gained sudden national fame thanks to a product having nothing to do with land. On this Monday, 20th, its inhabitants celebrate four months of the most extraordinary report of a direct third-degree contact between humans and an extraterrestrial being ever made in the country.

At 3:30 PM on a sunny Saturday, January 20th, three girls were walking down a path on a vacant lot in the Jardim Andere neighborhood, two kilometers from the city center, when one of them, Liliane Fátima Silva, 16, looked to her left and screamed. A strange creature, with three protuberances on its head and viscous skin, was about seven meters away, near the wall dividing the lot from a mechanic's shop. "It was crouching, with its arms stretched out between its legs," the girl remembers. "I saw the eyes first, huge and red." Afraid, Liliane turned her back, while her sister Valquíria, 14, and friend Kátia Andrade Xavier, 22, continued observing.

"It wasn't an animal or a person, it was a horrible thing," Kátia asserts, who works as a domestic servant and has three children. "It seemed dazed, it didn't make any noise," Valquíria completes. However, the creature made a slight head movement, and the three girls ran away. Forty minutes later, Liliane and Valquíria's mother, Lutza Helena Silva, 38, arrived at the vacant lot to investigate what had so frightened her daughters. She found nothing. The story took proportions because, apparently without any kind of communication with Liliane, Valquíria, and Kátia, the couple of rural workers, Ondina Augusta and Eurico Rodrigues, claimed to have seen, in the early morning of January 20th, an Unidentified Flying Object (UFO). They were sleeping in the 150-acre farm house located on the road linking Varginha to Três Corações when they were woken up by the noise of the animals.

"The cattle was running back and forth in the pasture in front of our window," Eurico recounts. "We looked up at the sky and saw a gray object, with a shape similar to a submarine, the size of a minibus, slowly flying over the pasture at five meters from the ground," Oralina describes. "It was releasing white smoke."

Luiz Cesário da Silveira Leite, professor of psychiatry at Harvard Medical School and researcher into human encounters with aliens.

The balcony lost time and attracts customers with figures
a creature identical to the stylized extraterrestrials. In the stationery
three months ago, in the center of Varginha, an ET made of Styrofoam, tissue paper and filled
with girls from the neighborhood. "I was stuck with old newspaper decorating the window and attracting
the attention of consumers." In 24 years of
business, he couldn't look away from those
horrible, wide-open eyes. "It's the ugliest thing I've ever seen, but it's what attracts the most people,"
celebrates the merchant José Maria da Silva, owner
of the stationery. He says, however, that his sales haven't increased. "People just want
to look at the ET." The creator of the doll,
in the midst of the controversy surrounding the ET, was his nephew, Alan Tempes, 17,
from Varginha. There is at least one certainty.
he doesn't believe in extraterrestrials. "I took advantage of the idea," says Tempes.

for the appearance of the extraterrestrial, no one would be talking about Varginha," evaluates the

Brazilian military, however, assured that only three

--- seg-6f9b3abe445d59e1c0ac4b3e [not-required] ---

--- seg-9e0588872887f97b989d2e63 [needs-review] ---
Lima promised an official report solely to deal with urological phenomena. Exactly ten years ago, however, the Air Force's investigations led to the deployment of three fighter jets for days. To this day, the results of that investigation are kept under seven seals, and three Mirage IIIs were sent out in pursuit of supposed UFOs (Unidentified Flying Objects). The evasive operation, headed by Colonel Cerqueira, the chief of the Air Defense Service, was triggered by the chief of the Flight Protection Service in São Paulo. Others, with better memories, avoid commenting on the investigation's outcome: "It was an exceptional occurrence, but we found no explanation," maintains Brigadier Moreira Lima. Sought for comment by IstoÉ in São José dos Campos, where he lives, and in São Paulo, where he works, the ex-minister Ozires Silva did not respond to the report. Despite official silence, ufologists do not intend to file this case away. The episode will be the subject of a book, currently in its final phase, by the president of the National Institute of Aerospace Phenomena Investigation (Infa), Claudeir Covo. At the time, Minister of Aeronautics Octávio Moreira Lima assured citizens the right to know about this case. "The UFOs were, at most, 20," said Colonel-aviator Ney Antunes Cerqueira, then head of the Air Defense Operations Center.

In the April 1996 issue of the CONTATO UPO journal from Editora Abril, a research conducted by researcher Ari João U. Rosas was published. Around 11:23 PM on April 14, 1996, Mr. Antonio Parciliano, 42, was driving with his family across the highway that connects the cities of Cravinhos and Bonifás Paulista, when they spotted a "strange object" in the sky. It was later found that the moonlight emitted by the UFO illuminated an area of 19 meters in diameter on the ground.

The feet of the coffee plants had their stems completely burned from the middle up, and the leaves, as if falling, were charred and twisted.

Curious facts that caught attention: from the middle down, the leaves and fruits remained green, while the branches, still bearing fruits, were burned on the upper side, but green on the lower side.

In the city, several people claimed to have seen strange lights in the days leading up to the Parciliano family's encounter.

According to researcher Piras' study, the UFO had bright yellow lights, 10 to 20 meters in diameter and 5 to 7 meters high, leaving four marks that sank into the ground 6.9 meters deep.

Dirceu was so frightened that he couldn't sleep for several nights following the incident.

Bridge: Journal DIÁRIO POPULAR, 02 of April 1.996. The Special Civil Judge of the Criminal Court of Brasília-DF, former Small Causes Court, received a curious denunciation. A farmer from the rural area of the Federal District, who identified himself only as João, filed a complaint against an extraterrestrial, who, according to him, was frightening his pig farm, especially the sows, which he had left to breed. According to the judge, Raacy Andrigue, from the 2nd Specialized Court, the rural producer, who did not present personal documents, appears to be approximately 65 years old and was visibly nervous while trying to describe the UFO's behavior, which he claims hovers over his farm every night. Last week, the judge said in an interview with the press that the court, established a few years ago, would seek to "resolve" all the problems of the population of DF. Farmer João took it literally. "We want to found the justice of the third millennium, we did not expect to start with complaints against them," the judge joked. The judge said she clarified to the farmer that to file a complaint, he needed to notify the responsible party, in this case, the extraterrestrial.

around 20 UFO sightings in that region during that period."

water reservoir, and other triangular objects joined it, all emitting multicolored
lights. In Pennsylvania, a bright disk was seen.

--- seg-27ab05b3888b72b88fc37bdc [needs-review] ---
*Unidentified Flying Object (UFO)*, as reported by witnesses — a phenomenon that has intrigued scientists and the public for decades. On this particular day, several individuals claimed to have seen a strange, disc-shaped object in the sky. The object was described as moving erratically, defying conventional aircraft maneuvers.

Multiple sightings were reported across different locations, creating a sense of mystery and excitement. Some observers described the UFO as having bright, pulsating lights, while others mentioned a silent, swift movement. The object's appearance and behavior sparked numerous theories, ranging from advanced military technology to extraterrestrial visitation.

As the news spread, a sense of curiosity and speculation grew. Scientists and researchers began to study the reports, trying to find patterns and explanations. Some suggested that the UFO sightings could be attributed to natural phenomena or optical illusions, while others proposed more extraordinary hypotheses.

The government, recognizing the potential impact of these events, decided to investigate. They established a task force to gather evidence and interview witnesses. The search for answers continued, but the true nature of the UFO sightings remained elusive.

Despite the lack of concrete evidence, the UFO phenomenon continues to captivate and divide opinion. Some believe it is a wake-up call for humanity to consider the possibility of extraterrestrial life, while others remain skeptical, attributing the sightings to human error or natural occurrences. The search for truth in the sky remains an ongoing quest.

Turning to this, we reiterate our requests from n° 5/113, of June 1st, 1999, and n° 96/086 of April 24th of this year (copies attached), as we have not received a response, we would like to request that the following be provided as assured by the Brazilian Constitution.

I.

I return to reiterate my request, as well as the citizens signed below, regarding the known case as "VABQINHA". We request that you clarify the fact, if there is a policy regarding the secrecy of this case or other related facts to the presence of UFOs or Extraterrestrial Vehicles (OVNI) archived at OONSABHl.

Also, we would like to clarify a doubt regarding the case of the "Halo" of 1986, when Brigadier Octavio Merisira Lina, former Minister of Aeronautics, appeared on TV, stating that Brazil "captured and recorded on radar the signals of the combat against more than 2 dozen OVHs located in Rio de Janeiro - SP".

It is disconcerting that the Ministry of Aeronautics has made two completely different statements regarding the case of MAIO/86. On August 18th, 1991, the Social Communication Office of the Aeronautics, through Letter n° 042/DJR, addressed to Mr. Rafael Ory of Curitiba-PR, informed that the episode was thoroughly researched and that the anomalies reported were magnetic "plests". It further stated that "the entire military apparatus of the air space was mobilized so that, effectively, there was no visual contact that would justify the presence of those (the) 'plests'". In this case, an explanation was given by Brigadier-General Fernando Mandos Nogueira, who signed the letter, a copy of which we hold.

On July 17th, 1996, the same Social Communication Office of the Aeronautics, through Letter n° 669/DSP, addressed to Brigadier-General Oladeir Ceva of São Paulo, informed that "the event that occurred on May 19th, 1986, after exhaustive examination and hearing all involved parties, it was not possible to reach a conclusion, therefore, the promised disclosure was not made, and since then, this Ministry considers the subject closed." The letter is signed by 1st Lieutenant-Air Force Jador da Silva Garcia, a copy of which we hold.

Well, what is happening with the Ministry of Aeronautics that it is not speaking the same language? Why in 1991 it was said that there was no visual contact, but then, the pilots appeared on television?

National Report - On the morning of January 20th, 10:30 AM, in front of number 3 on a street in the Jardim Andere neighborhood, Varginha, Minas Gerais, Brazil, a group of people witnessed unusual objects in the sky.

U.S. and Brazilian military forces, operating under a military cooperation agreement, were tracking these objects. One of the objects, apparently damaged by an explosion, was observed to have one of its ends structurally compromised. It slowly descended and landed near the farm, under the supervision of Major Maciel.

The creature, captured unharmed, was placed in a box, along with a network of debris from the object, and transported to a military base. According to some reports, it was a wooden structure, covered with a tarp, and the crew suffered minor injuries. Two soldiers were guarding the creature when a fire truck arrived at the scene.

## Translation of Text on Varginha Incident

**Strange Creatures -** To date, we have confirmed the capture of two beings, likely one of which received three shots from a FAL (assumed to be an alien weapon as EBEs (Biological Entities) confirmed by military personnel who participated and was sent dead to Unicamp, a university in Campinas, Brazil. Based on our current knowledge,

**Confusion in the Case -** To explain the extraterrestrial origin of these beings, much more intelligent than humans, a significant movement of military personnel occurred at ESA. Informants told us that on that day, a new recruit training session was held there, though this occurred in the week following.

**Explanations for the Military Movement -** Brazilian ufologists, below, will provide further information through the following contact: (011) 272-1441 or by mail at Caixa Postal 42.700, 04299-970, Ipiranga, São Paulo, SP.

Due to obvious reasons, please call from a public phone and do not give your name or address.

The vehicles, however, were sent to Automaco, a company, for balance adjustments and wheel alignment. They were not from any specific military unit and did not have any identifying marks.

**Automaco and the Incident -** Automaco does not operate on Saturdays and Sundays, the period in question.

**The Role of the Military in Hospitals -** To explain the significant military presence at the Regional Hospital, they cited the exhumation of a young man's body who had hanged himself in jail. According to the exhumation report, this occurred on January 30, 1996. The military movement occurred on January 20-22.

**Ufologists' Consensus -** This is a unique event in Brazil, whose confirmation could bring invaluable and unprecedented scientific knowledge, perhaps positive impacts of philosophical and cultural orders of magnitude.

**Military, Firefighters, and Police Involvement -** There is no clear explanation for why the military, firefighters, and police were involved in this process. The transfer of these advanced medical equipment raises questions.

--- seg-c604c932b2b7577b19626505 [needs-review] ---
Location and general characteristics of Papuda Prison

Located away from DF 001 highway, at the height of Km 4, is DF 465, the access road to the security area of Papuda Prison. It is 4 kilometers southeast of the Plano Piloto of Brasília, situated in a valley with an average altitude of 959 meters. Around it, the higher regions reach an altitude of 1,150 meters.

In the security area: the Internment and Reintegration Center - CIR, which housed, in April 1991, approximately 700 prisoners with sentences ranging from 10 to 15 years, the Brasília Custody Core - NCB, which, under the responsibility of the District Federal Civil Police, housed around 400 prisoners awaiting trial, and the 3rd Independent Police Company (3rd CPMInd), whose guard corps consisted of over 60 men.

It is a secluded location away from urban areas. Most of its area has no lighting. Inside the maximum security prison, CIR, lighting is provided.
--- seg-a941024602ddc48993072432 [needs-review] ---
made with 10 high-powered searchlights. It has 10 guardhouses for superior guard and guard posts on land.
It was in this environment that on April 11, 1991, from 7:10 pm to 10:40 pm,
an Unidentified Flying Object (UFO) was observed. The observation
was made by Lt. Damasceno and the soldiers who were on guard that night.
Approximately 25 police officers were present at the 3rd CPMInd. The rest of the
policing force that made up the guard that night were spread out at other missions
and guard posts.

What was observed
a
--- seg-d3d150256faa056d4fe484b6 [needs-review] ---
Lieutenant Damasceno left the 3rd CPMInd and ordered the driver, Sd Reinaldo, to prepare the vehicle for a routine patrol around the prison posts. Upon returning to the company, about 300 to 400 meters away, he noticed a strange object in the sky. He immediately called the guard to see it, with around 20 policemen present at that moment.

According to testimonies, the object constantly changed colors: blue, red, yellow, and a bit of green, occasionally flashing very brightly, turning the entire object red. Observations were made from three different positions all within the security perimeter. In addition to these observations, reports came in that there were other witnesses. We verified that these were personnel providing services in the nearby coal mines.

In the week following the collection of testimonies, we traveled to the coal mine region. The system of service provision with high turnover did not allow for clear identification of the object or further investigation.

===== PAGE 148 [page-500b712a2bc75ae9237e30da] =====

Find the witnesses. These had already returned to Minas Gerais, their state of origin, and others went to coal mines in the state of Mato Grosso. Observations from the coal mines would provide significant data as they represented an observation angle opposite to the others. When drawing the sketches, Sd Reinaldo requested that the "center of the object" be clearly defined, as it changed colors in different parts of its body at various moments, and was entirely taken over by red from the center.

Lieutenant Damasceno reviewed the sketch and confirmed its oval shape divided into colors. He noted that the apparent position of the object was vertical, and the speed of color change was too high to determine a sequence or order. "Red was the only color that did not suddenly appear and take over the entire object. The color change was very rapid." He added, "This is not the first time, nor the third time this has happened here. Talk to the prison staff and they will tell you how everyone is afraid of it."

The absence of a larger number of observers at different points and/or oppositely angled reduces the degree of precision in calculating distance, location, and the actual diameter of the object. However, additionally, the lack of documentary registration of the phenomenon (photos, videotapes, radar recordings) obviously hinders the scientific verification of certain propositions, as it compromises an in-depth study of the nature of the phenomenon. Nevertheless, the absence of these data does not diminish the credibility of testimonies that, along with documents from MAER, attest to the reality of the event.

Despite the insufficiency of data for more precise calculations, we made an estimate based on what we had, similar to the verification of calculation instruments, suggesting a real diameter of 20.50m for the observed form.

===== PAGE 149 [page-78fbb68d93c687be4db12962] =====
--- seg-65adab44b4658a4172053c45 [machine-unreviewed] ---
As for the object's movements, Ten. Damasceno states: "There was no movement; it was stationary, and we moved to a darker area to observe better. We returned to the courtyard, looked, and it was still there; suddenly, when we looked again, it was no longer there. We didn't see any movement... The object moved from its first position to northeast, returned to its initial position, and then moved to a third position further northeast and stayed there until roughly 22:40. The object disappeared from its first position, appeared in a second, returned to the first, and then reappeared in a third position, where it finally disappeared. There was no distraction, and I didn't see the movements." (DTI0,1 and 2)

Initially, the witness describes a sequence of disappearance, reappearance, and return.

The use of the terms "displacement" and "return" conveys the idea of movement that could have been witnessed during the displacement process. The terms "appeared," "disappeared," and "disappeared" align with his statement: "There was no distraction, and I didn't see the displacements" in such a way that the same pattern was observed in different positions.

"This is a typical characteristic of UFOs: according to numerous reports, they light up, turn off their lights, and then move. They light up again and are in a different position. In many cases, this behavior seems linked to a strategy to mislead the witness. Researcher Hulvio Brant Aleixo de Minas collected and reported several instances of this typical UFO behavior." (CARMO: 1991)

CINDACTA1: A necessary response? According to the testimony of Ten. Damasceno, during the observation period of the object, which took place from 7:10 p.m. to 10:40 p.m. approximately, there were 4 telephone contacts with CINDACTA I. Upon the first contact, Ten. Damasceno requested the object's identification. The first contact was made by Ten. Damasceno shortly before 8:00 p.m. The subsequent contacts were made by Sgt. Petrôni of CINDACTA I. In this regard, it can be said that CINDACTA I showed interest in the incident, predisposing to follow-up from the request of the officer in charge of the prison guard. However, after several attempts, it decided on the easiest way out: "Lieutenant, it was just a mess." To the indignation of the witnesses, this was the necessary response; categorical and objective, it should have been the position accepted by the prison security. A response intended to calm down, to regain the usual state of tranquility required by the system. The contradictions in the received documentation justify the inconsistency of the response given by Sgt. Petrôni of CINDACTA I to Ten. Damasceno (CPMInd). It also shows that within the MAER there is no consensus on how to handle this issue.

Thus, the indignation of the witnesses can be expressed: CINDACTA represents the eyes of the air defense system and air traffic control, and at that moment, due to the difficulties in clarification, it pretends to be short-sighted, rendering the efficiency of its radars useless. Despite having the telephone confirmation of the radar recording, no fighter jet was activated to investigate what it was.

Damasceno, though indignant, agreed with the difficulties of sending a fighter jet from one of the nearest air bases. His experience in dispatching vehicles for community calls related to police incidents allowed him to understand the problem. However, nothing could make him admit that what was observed could be a meteorological balloon.

Petrônio." According to Capt. Damasceno, there was a moment when Sgt. Petrônio spoke of the difficulty the Ministry of Aeronautics would have in explaining the case to the public opinion.
--- seg-8cd3b17c150fa4ca0ec57c1c [machine-unreviewed] ---
Contradictions in the information
--- seg-047c8edeeaac1d0ed7180111 [needs-review] ---
The clarification note from the Social Communication Center of the Ministry of Aeronautics - CECOMSAER, confirms in item I, the "observation of a signal that, processed by the computers of that center (CENDACTA I), was not characterized as any aircraft operating in the area."

The time the photo was taken (19:45) by CINDACTAI falls within the observation period according to witness statements. It does not coincide with the time of launch of the meteorological balloon on that date by CMA-BR, formerly CM I, as per OF No. 020/CMDO/020 of July 10, 1991. This document states the launch of that date at 21:00. The maximum altitude reached was 24,442 meters, under clear skies, with a maximum wind speed of 230750 knots, equivalent to 92.6 km/h. This indicates that a record of an object was made, and this object was neither a meteorological balloon nor a conventional aircraft.

It can be observed that prior to the launch of the balloon, the object was already being observed and recorded by CINDACTA I at 19:45.

The same clarification note, item 2, states that there was a coincidence in the record of the launch of a meteorological balloon by (the former) CM-1, suggesting the possibility that the meteorological balloon was being recorded. This suggestion, as we have seen, is not valid, as there was not a complete coincidence of times, the launch of that date was at 21:00, and the observation of the phenomenon was from 19:10 to 22:40.

Item 3 presents no inconsistencies.

However, item 4 of the same note presents a partial inconsistency regarding the association of the characteristics of meteorological balloons with the characteristics of UFOs. Such a generalization cannot be made, as meteorological balloons are easily distinguishable from UFOs in most cases. According to many reports and documents (videotapes), UFOs perform sudden, high-speed maneuvers, disappear and reappear, and the color variations in this case, depending on the observation time, have little to do with solar reflections on the balloons' surfaces, as stated in OF No. 020/CMDO/020 and witness statements.

The questionnaire used to collect information about UFOs presents precise and objective questions aimed at solid evidence of the phenomenon.

The observed and reported characteristics generally indicate that the UFO phenomenon is furtive and generates complex social interaction. This makes necessary a more detailed questionnaire that addresses the peculiarities and complexities of the phenomenon.

It is therefore necessary to assess its objective and subjective characteristics. In this regard, there is no perceived interest from the Ministry of Aeronautics. The report only serves to evaluate aerial sightings of a more common type.

Thus, it seems that although recognizing the existence of the phenomenon through the creation of a data capture instrument, its ultimate purpose is not to know the nature of the phenomenon in its entirety. It only aims to know technical data that may interfere with the operation of its functions in real time.

Thus, a large amount of data is left aside regarding the phenomenon itself and the social issues arising from it.

Characteristics of the Meteorological Balloon used on 11/04/91
According to oficio 020/CMDO/020, the meteorological balloon used on the night of
11/04/91 is a balloon manufactured in Japan by the companies KKS, measuring 1.20m in diameter, and
the material used is cosmo-prene plastic. It weighs 350g and carries 200g of equipment
intended to collect data on temperature, direction, and air speed in the upper atmosphere.
It can reach an altitude of 25,000m and can be carried by winds reaching speeds
"equal to or greater than 130kf. (130 knots - 210.76 km/h)
Furthermore, according to the aforementioned source, this balloon is inflated with hydrogen and
does not carry lights.

--- seg-472637952f56b8a9f39570a0 [needs-review] ---
Conclusion?

Claiming ignorance of the nature of the UFO phenomenon in no way should contradict any public or private institution. However, we believe it is necessary to sincerely explore our limitations, engage in discussion, and clarify positions regarding this topic.

In the case of MAER, its positions are so intertwined that it becomes difficult to determine if it has investigative functions for such events, or what its role is regarding this subject matter.

Are investigative functions related to the UFO topic defined as a regulatory responsibility for a MAER body?

Social expectations regarding MAER are such that, given its interest in airspace (the predominant arena for UFO activity) and its resources in terms of equipment and personnel, the possibility that its regulations do not provide for investigation procedures for such occurrences is not even considered. In light of the confusion mentioned above, it is difficult, even for us, to understand the reasons behind seemingly well-intentioned statements:

"Regarding Air Defense, its purpose is, in principle, to secure and real-time monitoring of airspace. The occurrences now under discussion are difficult to clarify because they are reported a posteriori, and provide little opportunity for a more accurate evaluation" (Jaeckel, Ronald Eduardo - Chief of Staff, COMGAR: 1993)

The fact is that, in this case, the communication was made in real-time, allowing for safe monitoring. So, why the denial of registration or the association with meteorological balloons, among so many other contradictions?

The sincerity and support of authorities leading the mentioned institutions could allow society, through the involvement of various segments dealing with this topic, to assess the actual needs and the appropriate treatment for the ufological problem, including legislative or regulatory measures. This is about pointing directions and responsibilities for solving and clarifying a situation that today constitutes a growing social problem.

Numerous researchers, in their statements, have consistently demanded a position from the Ministry of Aeronautics regarding the ufoological issue. Such demands seem to indicate disagreement with existing positions.

===== PAGE 154 [page-08af2fa4e65fcfb072c51a8b] =====
--- seg-0936ed2606b98cc99df624e1 [machine-unreviewed] ---
issued by MAER, as mentioned above, and a call for a change in
positioning that would mean a commitment to clarifying the UFO phenomenon.

There is a sense of fragility regarding the UFO phenomenon and a need for institutional support to conduct more comprehensive and in-depth research.

UFO Capital
In the Patos Lagoon region (RS), witnesses report 30 sightings in three months, and in the most spectacular one, businessman Haroldo Wes, a gaucho, claims to have seen a mother ship. The shock was immense. Even a weathered gallon, 39, runs a company that processes 7,300 tons of feed a year. Married for 14 years and the father of a nine-year-old, he remained for over ten minutes. His statement, given at a speed of 60 miles per hour (about 100 km/h) and at an altitude of 1,800 meters, was fantastic. During the time

# Contra Fatos Não Há Argumentos

## A Lua e a Ufologia

Em 1969, os repórteres cobraram que a ciência explicasse 37 anos-luz de viagem espacial, mas a ufologia é rica em fatos que desafiam explicações convencionais. É mais fácil negar do que provar a existência de milhares de exemplos entre as 300 estrelas observáveis. Cientistas, temerosos de parecerem ridículos, inventaram a palavra "impossível" para descrever o que veem como fenômenos ufológicos.

## Controle Gravitacional e Viagens Espaciais

Os russos já passaram meses no espaço, demonstrando que o controle gravitacional é possível. Einstein discutiu a unificação dos campos, sugerindo que o controle gravitacional é uma realidade eminente. A Nasa gasta fortunas em pesquisas, afirmando que a vida é um privilégio do planeta Terra no cosmos, mas muitos astrônomos e físicos acreditam que a vida extraterrestre é uma possibilidade real.

## Erros do Passado e Avanços Tecnológicos

No passado, a ideia de luz elétrica era absurda para muitos dentistas, mas Thomas Alva Edison a tornou realidade. Da mesma forma, o voo a jato, que parecia um sonho há 100 anos, é agora uma realidade. A tecnologia avança, e a viagem espacial não é mais um conceito distante.

## Discos Voadores e Realidade

O fenômeno UFO mostra como será o nosso futuro: controle da matéria, teletransporte, viagens a outros sistemas estelares. Os discos voadores revelam essas possibilidades, mas alguns dentistas céticos negam a realidade deles, alegando que são sonhos ou fantasias. No entanto, a tecnologia avança, e a viagem espacial não é mais um conceito distante.

## O Destino da Humanidade e Viajantes Espaciais

Será que, em 100.000 anos, as gerações futuras olharão para trás e se perguntarão como era a vida na Terra? Viajantes espaciais de civilizações avançadas podem estar entre nós, visitando-nos através de discos voadores, enquanto a humanidade ainda luta para entender seu próprio destino.

For astronomer Roberto Boczko (smaller photo), the object recorded in this smoke cannot be a celestial body.

This film was taken on May 23, 1386, by the team from Miksom, from the building of the State Bank of São Paulo, at the beginning of São João Avenue. So far, all analyses have confirmed that it is a genuine flying saucer, in the shape of a sphere, with a diameter of 6 to 8 meters. The object was approximately 10km away, over Cantareira Mountain, and was even detected by São Paulo's radar.

Astronomer Roberto Boczko's testimony about the film is of great importance as it rules out the possibility of a mistake with a celestial body. He stated:

"The closest similarity would be with the moon, but since it is filmed in another position, on the right, then it cannot be."

"No planet has such a bright glow."

"No star in that position has that kind of glow."

"Unless it were some kind of anomalous refraction, a mirage that makes the image appear in a position where it is not really,"

"This object should not be confused with any celestial body, and the explanation should be sought in some other field and not in astronomy."
